清单（gen 组批 §11 登记）：
1. **DyePlants**（plantDye 染料植物种植/GrowGlowTulips 荧光郁金香/PlaceChilletEggs）——读原版 pass 定位（WorldGen AddGenerationPass 列表找 DyePlants 注册段），1:1 移植到管线正确位置。
2. **SurfaceOreAndStone**（地表矿石与石块散布）。
3. **FallenLogsAndWaterFeatures**（倒木与水景——MicroBiomes 部分已有 Campsite/MiningExplosives，这里是 FallenLog 倒木+水景）。
4. **LongMoss**（长苔藓藤）。
5. **LilypadsCattailsBambooAndSeaweed**（睡莲/香蒲/竹/海藻——我们可能已有部分 BeachDecor？grep 现状去重）。
6. **Vines**（自然藤蔓垂直生长）。
7. **Flowers**（地表花——SurfaceDecor 部分已有杂草/花？去重核对）。
8. **Mushrooms**（地表蘑菇——同上去重）。

方法：先读原版 AddGenerationPass 完整注册表（WorldGen.cs 11525-22660 区段已有对照表 docs/worldgen/vanilla-pass-alignment.md——先读这个文档！）确认每个 pass 的注册序/原始方法行号，再读原文逐个移植。**必须按原版注册序插入管线**（WorldGen.ts passes 数组）。与既有 surfacePass/vanillaSurfaceDecor/vanillaBeachDecor 的重叠部分先 grep 去重（已有实现就不重复放，缺的补）。

验证：每个 pass 移植后跑 seed-parity/生成探针（5 种子全量生成）；vitest 全量保持绿（含 caves-checkpoint——改动在链后应无影响）；tsc 零新增。报告 ≤30 行：每 pass 状态（已移植 x 行/已有去重/依赖登记）+ 注册序对照 + 回归结果。

- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch

192	function vanillaTerrain({ store, rng, world, surface, cfg }: GenCtx) {
193	  const gs = newGenState(store.w, store.h);
194	  // 世界种子 int——DesertHive 的 FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(57005) 主种子
195	  // （DesertHive.cs:253，与 genRand 完全独立的第二条随机流）
196	  gs.seed = translateSeed(cfg.seedText);
197	  // ===== Reset 掷骰（1456 WorldGen.generateWorld cs:11159-11472 逐行 1:1）=====
198	  // 原版在 AddPasses 循环之前按此固定顺序消耗 Main.rand（种子等价的流位置契约）。
199	  // 天气/背景/月亮等值我们未消费——照掷照弃，只保留 GenState 下游字段。
200	  const W = store.w;
201	  // ① jungleHut（cs:11179）——丛林小屋样式映射，未消费
202	  rng.nextInt(5);
203	  // ② crimsonLeft（cs:11235）——邪恶群系左右分布，未消费
204	  rng.nextInt(2);
205	  // ③ RandomizeWeather（cs:7736-7746）：numClouds + 风速 do-while
206	  rng.nextIntRange(10, 200);
207	  for (;;) {
208	    const wf = rng.next();              // NextFloat = (float)NextDouble（1 次采样）
209	    rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);             // Next(2)（符号）
210	    if (wf !== 0) break;                // ×0.35×(±1) 非零 ⇔ wf≠0
211	  }
212	  // ④ 地狱箱主件洗牌（cs:11262-11274）：Next(5..1) 逐个抽出 → gs.hellChestItems
213	  {
214	    const pool = [274, 220, 112, 218, 3019];
215	    const out: number[] = [];
216	    while (pool.length > 0) out.push(...pool.splice(rng.nextInt(pool.length), 1));
217	    gs.hellChestItems = out;
218	  }
219	  // ⑤⑥ slimeRainTime / cloudBGActive（cs:11277-11283，天气初值，未消费）
220	  rng.nextIntRange(86400 * 2, 86400 * 3);

222	  // ⑦-⑩ 矿石替代对（cs:11286-11298）——Next(2)==0 用替代矿
223	  gs.oreTiers = {

226	    silver: rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_tungsten']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_silver']!,
227	    gold: rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_platinum']! : TILE_BY_KEY['ore_gold']!,
228	  };
229	  // ⑪ crimson（cs:11321）+ UI 覆盖（骰照掷仅覆盖结果，cs:11326-11332）
230	  gs.crimson = rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0;
231	  if (cfg.evil === 0) gs.crimson = false;
232	  else if (cfg.evil === 1) gs.crimson = true;
233	  world.crimson = gs.crimson;
234	  // ⑫ WorldId（cs:11354）——Next(int.MaxValue) 单次采样，未消费

237	  const ts = randomizeTreeStyle(rng, W);
238	  gs.treeX = ts.treeX;
239	  gs.treeStyle = ts.treeStyle;

241	  rng.nextIntRange(1050, 3150);                            // caveBackX[0]
242	  {
243	    const a = rng.nextInt(8);
244	    let b = rng.nextInt(8);
245	    while (b === a) b = rng.nextInt(8);
246	  }
247	  // ⑮ RandomizeBackgrounds（cs:7913-7960，全部未消费照掷）
248	  {
249	    const rollForest = () => {
250	      let n = rng.nextInt(14);
251	      if ((n === 1 || n === 2) && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) n = rng.nextInt(14);
252	      if (n === 0) n = rng.nextInt(14);

262	    while (bg3 === bg1 || bg3 === bg2) bg3 = rollForest();

264	    while (bg4 === bg1 || bg4 === bg2 || bg4 === bg3) bg4 = rollForest();
265	    { // Corruption 背景：Next(6)；==5 时 Next(2) 定 51/52
266	      const n = rng.nextInt(6);
267	      if (n === 5) rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);
268	    }
269	    rng.nextInt(7);                                        // setBG(2, jungle)
270	    const snowBG = rng.nextInt(9);
271	    if (snowBG === 2 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);
272	    if (snowBG === 3 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);
273	    if (snowBG === 4 && rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0) rng.nextIntRange(0, 2);
274	    rng.nextInt(6);                                        // setBG(4, hallow)
275	    rng.nextInt(7);                                        // setBG(5, crimson)
276	    { // Desert 背景：Next(6)；==5 时 Next(5)
277	      const n = rng.nextInt(6);
278	      if (n === 5) rng.nextInt(5);
279	    }
280	    rng.nextInt(8);                                        // setBG(7, ice)
281	    rng.nextInt(5);                                        // setBG(8, mushroom)
282	    rng.nextInt(3);                                        // setBG(9, underworld)
283	  }
284	  // ⑯ RandomizeMoonState（cs:7748）——Next(9)，未消费
285	  rng.nextInt(9);
286	  // （TreeTops.CopyExistingWorldInfoForWorldGeneration / SetupDungeonGenVars：零掷骰）
287	  // ⑰ dungeonSide（cs:11360）：Next(2)==0 → Left(我们 -1)
288	  gs.dungeonSide = rng.nextIntRange(0, 2) === 0 ? -1 : 1;
289	  // ⑱ 丛林（cs:11377-11392）：与地牢异侧，Next(15,30)
290	  if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) {
291	    gs.jungleX = Math.trunc(W * (1 - rng.nextIntRange(15, 30) * 0.01));
292	  } else {
293	    gs.jungleX = Math.trunc(W * (rng.nextIntRange(15, 30) * 0.01));
294	  }
295	  world.jungleX = gs.jungleX;
296	  // ⑲ 地牢侧锚点 num7（cs:11394-11416）：Next(w) 拒绝采样到地牢侧 15% 区间
297	  let dX = rng.nextInt(W);
298	  const dLo = W * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.60 : 0.25);
299	  const dHi = W * (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 0.75 : 0.40);
300	  while (dX < dLo || dX > dHi) dX = rng.nextInt(W);
301	  gs.dungeonX = dX;
302	  // ⑳ 雪原（cs:11425-11440）：锚点两侧独立扩展（num9 = w/4200，小世界恰 1.0）
303	  const num9 = W / 4200;
304	  const snowHalf = () => rng.nextIntRange(50, 90)
305	    + Math.trunc(rng.nextIntRange(20, 40) * num9)
306	    + Math.trunc(rng.nextIntRange(20, 40) * num9);
307	  const snowL = snowHalf(), snowR = snowHalf();
308	  gs.snowOriginLeft = Math.max(0, dX - snowL);
309	  gs.snowOriginRight = Math.min(W, dX + snowR);
310	  // ㉑㉒ 海滩（cs:11445-11459）：Next(300,340)（center 320±range 20）+ 侧别加宽
311	  gs.beachLeftEnd = rng.nextIntRange(300, 340) + (gs.dungeonSide === 1 ? 40 : 20);
312	  gs.beachRightStart = W - rng.nextIntRange(300, 340) - (gs.dungeonSide === -1 ? 40 : 20);
313	  // ㉓ 地牢入口（cs:11461-11470）：地牢侧海滩内 15% 区间（dungeonBeachPadding=50）
314	  if (gs.dungeonSide === -1) {
315	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.nextIntRange(gs.beachLeftEnd + 50, Math.trunc(W * 0.2));
316	  } else {
317	    gs.dungeonLocation = rng.nextIntRange(Math.trunc(W * 0.8), gs.beachRightStart - 50);
318	  }
319	  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonLocation;

321	  runTerrainPass(store, rng, gs);

323	  for (let x = 0; x < store.w; x++) {
324	    for (let y = 0; y < store.h; y++) {
325	      if (store.flags[store.idx(x, y)]) { surface[x] = y; break; }
326	    }
327	  }
328	  ctxGs[0] = gs;

330	  world.groundLevel = gs.worldSurface;
331	  world.rockLevel = gs.rockLevel;
332	  world.lavaLine = gs.lavaLine;
333	}

725	function placeAlch(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {

730	  if (!st.flags[g] || st.half[g] || st.slope[g] !== 0) return false;
731	  if (!ALCH_GROUND[style].has(st.type[g])) return false;
732	  const liq = st.liquid[k];
733	  const lava = liq > 0 && st.liquidType[k] === 2;
734	  if (style <= 3) { if (liq > 0) return false; }
735	  else if (style === 4 || style === 6) { if (lava) return false; }
736	  else if (style === 5) { if (liq > 0 && !lava) return false; }
737	  st.setTileSilent(x, y, HERB_SPROUT, 18 * style, 0);
738	  return true;
739	}
740	
741	/** PlantAlch（cs:45910）1:1：y 带三段掷（1/40 深层带、9/10 地表带、其余全域），
742	 *  下落至首个活性格；基座须 nactive 且上格净空无液；±15（×w/4200）内草药 <5 才种 */
743	function plantAlch(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
744	  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
745	  const num = rng.int(20, w - 21);                        // Next(20, maxTilesX-20)
746	  let y: number;
747	  if (rng.int(0, 39) === 0) y = rng.int(Math.floor((gs.rockLevel + h) / 2), h - 21);
748	  else if (rng.int(0, 9) !== 0) y = rng.int(Math.floor(gs.worldSurface), h - 21);
749	  else y = rng.int(20, h - 21);
750	  while (y < h - 20 && !(st.inBounds(num, y) && st.flags[st.idx(num, y)])) y++;
751	  if (!st.inBounds(num, y)) return;
752	  const k = st.idx(num, y);
753	  if (!st.flags[k] || st.half[k] || st.slope[k] !== 0) return;   // nactive
754	  if (!st.inBounds(num, y - 1)) return;
755	  if (st.flags[st.idx(num, y - 1)] || st.liquid[st.idx(num, y - 1)] !== 0) return;
756	  const rad = Math.floor(15 * (w / 4200));
757	  const x0 = Math.max(4, Math.min(w - 4, num - rad));
758	  const x1 = Math.max(4, Math.min(w - 4, num + rad));
759	  const y0 = Math.max(4, Math.min(h - 4, y - rad));
760	  const y1 = Math.max(4, Math.min(h - 4, y + rad));
761	  let near = 0;
762	  for (let i = x0; i <= x1; i++) {
763	    for (let j = y0; j <= y1; j++) {
764	      if (TILE_ALCH.has(st.type[st.idx(i, j)])) near++;
765	    }
766	  }
767	  if (near < 5) {
768	    for (const [set, style] of HERB_STYLE_GROUND) {
769	      if (set.has(st.type[k])) placeAlch(st, num, y - 1, style);
770	    }
771	  }
772	}
773	
774	/** AlchemyHerbs pass（cs:20109）：w×1.7 次 PlantAlch */
775	export function runAlchemyHerbsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
776	  const count = Math.floor(st.w * 1.7);
777	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) plantAlch(st, rng, gs);
778	}
779	
780	/** MatureTheHerbPlants（cs:45757，DyePlants pass 内调用）1:1：
781	 *  岩层以下泥土/泥面 1/25 补种 82(frameX=36)；既有 82 → 1/3 熟成 83，
782	 *  frameX=36 再 1/2、frameX=108 再 1/3 进阶 84 */
783	export function matureHerbPlants(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
784	  for (let i = 10; i < st.w - 10; i++) {
785	    for (let j = 10; j < st.h - 10; j++) {
786	      const k = st.idx(i, j);
787	      const below = st.idx(i, j + 1);
788	      if (j > gs.rockLevel && (st.type[below] === MUD_TILE || st.type[below] === T.DIRT)
789	        && _solidTileHB(st, i, j + 1) && !st.flags[k] && st.liquid[k] === 0
790	        && rng.int(0, 24) === 0) {
791	        st.setTileSilent(i, j, HERB_SPROUT, 36, 0);
792	      }
793	      if (st.type[k] === HERB_SPROUT && rng.int(0, 2) === 0) {
794	        st.type[k] = HERB_MATURE;
795	        if (st.frameX[k] === 36 && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) st.type[k] = HERB_BLOOM;
796	        if (st.frameX[k] === 108 && rng.int(0, 2) === 0) st.type[k] = HERB_BLOOM;
797	      }
798	    }
799	  }
800	}
801

347	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;

349	  let x = rng.int(6, 14);
350	  let sinceLast = 0;
351	  while (x < w - 6) {

// 逐 pass 网格哈希检查点（种子等价 L1 工具）：
// generateWorld 的 onWorldPartial 在每个 pass 后回调——对 type/flags/wall/liquid
// 四数组做 FNV-1a，输出「pass 名 → 哈希」清单。C# oracle 逐段延伸后，
// 本清单与 oracle 各段哈希逐项比对即可二分定位首个分歧 pass。
// 跑法：npx vitest run tests/pass-hash.test.ts（控制台输出哈希表）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';

export function fnv1a(arr: ArrayLike<number>, mod = 2 ** 32): string {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
    h ^= arr[i] & 0xff;
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
    h ^= (arr[i] >>> 8) & 0xff;
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
    // Uint16Array 上 16 位全吃；Uint8Array 高 8 位自然为 0
  }
  return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

describe('逐 pass 网格哈希检查点', () => {
  it('种子 9293480：每 pass 后 type/flags/wall/liquid FNV 哈希', async () => {
    const checkpoints: Array<{ pass: string; hash: string }> = [];
    const world = await generateWorld({
      width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,
      onWorldPartial: (w, _idx, name) => {
        const st = w.store;
        checkpoints.push({
          pass: name,
          hash: [fnv1a(st.type), fnv1a(st.flags), fnv1a(st.wall), fnv1a(st.liquid)].join(' '),
        });
      },
    });
    const st = world.store;
    checkpoints.push({ pass: 'finalize', hash: [fnv1a(st.type), fnv1a(st.flags), fnv1a(st.wall), fnv1a(st.liquid)].join(' ') });
    for (const c of checkpoints) console.log(`${c.pass.padEnd(6, '　')} ${c.hash}`);
    expect(checkpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  }, 600000);
});

function hashGrids(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; liquid: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; w: number; h: number }) {
  const n = st.w * st.h;
  const mapped = new Uint16Array(n);
  const ghost = new Uint16Array(n);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const v = V_ID[st.type[i]];
    if (st.flags[i]) mapped[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
    else ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v; // 幽灵 parity：inactive 格的残留 type
  }
  return `${fnv1a(mapped)} ${fnv1a(st.flags)} ${fnv1a(st.liquid)}`;
}

/** wall 单独哈希——只对沙漠段启用：此前 pass 的 JS 墙与 oracle 有既知偏差

 *  以墙(187/216)为核心输出，从这里开始逐哈希比对。 */
function hashWalls(st: { wall: Uint16Array }): string {
  return fnv1a(st.wall);
}

export function runShimmerPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
  const ws = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface), rl = Math.floor(gs.rockLevel);
  // cs:15259-15268:深度带
  const yLoBase = Math.trunc((ws + rl) / 2) + 50;
  let yHi = Math.trunc(((h - 250) * 2 + rl) / 3);
  if (yHi > h - 330 - 100 - 30) yHi = h - 330 - 100 - 30;
  if (yHi <= yLoBase) yHi = yLoBase + 50;
  const pickX = (wide: boolean): number =>
    gs.dungeonSide < 1
      ? rng.int(Math.trunc(w * (wide ? 0.8 : 0.89)), w - 201)
      : rng.int(200, Math.trunc(w * (wide ? 0.2 : 0.11)) - 1);
  let x = pickX(false);
  let y = rng.int(yLoBase, yHi - 1);
  let tries = 0;
  while (!shimmerMakeBiome(st, rng, gs, x, y)) {
    tries++;
    if (tries > 20000) {
      // cs:15292-15295:放宽 x 带(0.8w/w-200 或 200/0.2w),y 下限 ws+100+20
      y = rng.int(ws + 100 + 20, yHi - 1);
      x = pickX(true);
    } else {
      // cs:15297-15300:y 下限 (ws+rl)/2+20,x 带不变
      y = rng.int(Math.trunc((ws + rl) / 2) + 20, yHi - 1);
      x = pickX(false);
    }
  }
  gs.shimmerX = x;
  gs.shimmerY = y;
}

/** cs:34889-35056 ShimmerMakeBiome 主体 */

  const n5 = rng.int(105, 124); // Next(105,125)
  const n6 = Math.trunc(n5 * n4);
  const n7 = Math.trunc(n5 * n2);

// 原版 WorldGen.TileRunner 移植(WorldGen.cs:46405)——地形/洞穴/矿石/沙/泥的万能 workhorse。
// 沿随机方向走 steps 步,每步以衰减半径的钻石区域(L1 距离 + 1.5% 抖动)铺设/挖除 type。
// type<0 = 挖空(-1 空气 / -2 遇活性块则填液体);type>=0 = 铺设(仅改写活性块 type,
// addTile 才激活空气格——原版语义,空气格上的幽灵 type 不渲染)。
import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';

const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;
const CLAY = TILE_BY_KEY['clay']!;
const SANDSTONE = TILE_BY_KEY['sandstone']!;
const HARDSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand']!;
const MARBLE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_367_marble_block']!;
const GRANITE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_368_granite_block']!;

/** 按 vanilla sheet id 收集本仓库 tile id(缺失的自动跳过) */
export function idsByVanillaSheet(...sheets: number[]): Set<number> {
  const out = new Set<number>();
  for (const d of TILE_DEFS) {
    const vs = d.vanilla?.sheet;
    if (vs !== undefined && sheets.includes(vs)) out.add(TILE_BY_KEY[d.key]);
  }
  return out;
}

// 原版 Main.tileStone(Main.cs:6120-6139):宝石 63-68 + 活/失活石 130/131 + 琥珀石 566。
// 只有这些"放置类型"触发 flag3(仅替换石头)
const STONE_PLACED = idsByVanillaSheet(63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68, 130, 131, 566);
// TileID.Sets.CanBeClearedDuringGeneration = false 全集（1456 TileID.cs:321，
// 2026-08-11 种子等价对齐——曾自加电路器件 135-137/141/443 保护，原版生成期可覆盖）
export const NOT_CLEARABLE = idsByVanillaSheet(
  396, 400, 401, 397, 398, 399, 404,   // 沙岩家族
  368, 367,                            // 花岗岩/大理石
  41, 43, 44,                          // 地牢砖
  481, 482, 483,                       // 1.4.5 新增不可清块
  226, 237);                           // 蜥蜴砖/陨石

  speedY?: number;

  ignoreTileType?: number; // 遇此类型跳过(-1 不过滤)
  mudWall?: boolean;     // 丛林泥墙模式(墙 15/64 按深度)
}

function animRot(): number {
  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / 6);

/** 渲染帧共享态:由 Renderer 每帧刷新(chunk 烘焙期读取) */
export const renderEnv = {
  /** Main.WindForVisuals 近似 = 天气当前风速 */
  wind: 0,
  /** Main.worldSurface(DoesWindBlowAtThisHeight 用) */
  worldSurface: 0,
};
/** 428 测重板被踩状态(Game.triggerTiles428 镜像;原版 PressurePlateHelper.PressurePlatesPressed) */
export const plates428Pressed = new Set<number>();

/** WorldGen.InAPlaceWithWind(:87603):高度在 worldSurface 之上且区域内无液体无墙
 *  (AllowsWind 墙集合未提取,任何墙挡风为保守近似) */
function inAPlaceWithWind(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w2: number, h2: number): boolean {
  if (y >= renderEnv.worldSurface) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < w2; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < h2; j++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x + i, y + j)) continue;
      const ti = st.idx(x + i, y + j);
      if (st.liquid[ti] > 0 || st.wall[ti] > 0) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

/** 生物群系变体(529/530 的 GetBiomeInfluence 近似:看正下方格所属邪恶群系;海滩看 x) */
function biomeVariant529(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w: number): number {
  const CORRUPT = new Set(['v_25_ebonstone_block', 'v_23_corrupt_grass_block', 'v_112_ebonsand_block']);
  const CRIMSON = new Set(['v_203_crimstone_block', 'v_199_crimson_grass_block', 'v_234_crimsand_block']);
  const HALLOW = new Set(['v_117_pearlstone_block', 'v_109_hallowed_grass_block', 'v_116_pearlsand_block']);
  if (st.inBounds(x, y + 1)) {
    const k = TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(x, y + 1)]]?.key ?? '';
    if (HALLOW.has(k)) return 2;
    if (CRIMSON.has(k)) return 3;
    if (CORRUPT.has(k)) return 4;
  }
  const beach = 380; // WorldGen.beachDistance 近似
  return x < beach || x > w - beach ? 1 : 0;
}

PlaceTile also has pre-gates: the tile at (i,j) must be empty (Collision.EmptyTile) since 227 isn't solid — EmptyTile = !active. plantDye already ensures tile[i,j-1] inactive in the top part, but in the underground part (`else if` chain after line 71390) the check is again "!tile[i,j-1].active() || type in {3,51,61,73,74,184}". /** 绘制一格原版图块。
 *  auto：现场算帧；style：显式 18px 帧偏移。
 *  重要修正：wld 里多格家具每格 frameX 往往全 0（不是 0/18 交错），
 *  直接用存储帧会让 2 格宽家具两格都画锚点帧（重复左侧贴图）。
 *  style 模式改为：若本格帧偏移为 0，向左/上扫描同类同物体格子，
 *  按到锚点的格距重算帧偏移（锚点=左侧或上方最近格） */
export function drawVanillaCell(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,
  sheet: number, frame: 'auto' | 'style' | 'blend', fw: number, fh: number,
  st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number,
  same: (t: number) => boolean, px: number, py: number, fx: number, fy: number,
  trees?: TreeStyleData,
): void {
  // 751/752(睡着的掘地龟/啮龙蛋):仅锚点帧 (0,0) 绘制,非锚点格整格跳过(:1034-1048)
  if ((sheet === 751 || sheet === 752) && (fx !== 0 || fy !== 0)) return;
  // 树木族（tile 5 + 宝石树 583-589 + 装饰树 596/616 + 灰烬树 634 + 棕榈 323 + 蘑菇树 72）：
  // 树冠/树枝标记帧 + 生物群系树干（TEdit 同一套逻辑）。
  // 棕榈干身 frameY 是倾斜偏移（跨列）——主遍跳过，整棵由树族外扩遍绘制防裁剪
  if (frame === 'style' && (TREE_SHEET_STYLES[sheet] !== undefined || sheet === 323)) {
    if (sheet !== 323) drawTreeCell(ctx, atlas, st, x, y, fx, fy, px, py, sheet, false, trees);
    return;
  }
  // 仙人掌（tile 80）：wld 无帧，按邻接现场分帧 + 生物群系变体
  if (sheet === 80 && frame === 'style') {
    drawCactusCell(ctx, atlas, st, x, y, type, px, py);
    return;
  }
  // blend 类（泥土/石族/草族/沙/泥/雪）：TEdit BlendRules 移植
  // （掩码位序：E=0x1 N=0x10 W=0x100 S=0x1000；角 TR/TL/BL/BR <<16）
  if (frame === 'blend') {
    const myMeta = atlas.vmeta(sheet);
    const myMerge = myMeta?.mergeWith ?? null;
    const myStone = !!myMeta?.isStone;
    const nMeta = (t: number) => {
      const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
      return d?.vanilla ? atlas.vmeta(d.vanilla.sheet) : null;
    };
    // Merges() 链（TEdit TileProperty.Merges）：对方 mergeWith 指向我 / 我指向对方 / 双方同指
    const merges = (ot: number) => {
      if (ot === type) return true;
      const om = nMeta(ot);
      if (!om) return false;
      const oid = TILE_DEFS[ot]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
      if (om.mergeWith != null && om.mergeWith === sheet) return true;
      if (myMerge != null && myMerge === oid) return true;
      if (myMerge != null && om.mergeWith != null && myMerge === om.mergeWith) return true;
      return false;
    };
    const dirs: Array<[number, number, number]> = [
      [1, 0, 0x1], [0, -1, 0x10], [-1, 0, 0x100], [0, 1, 0x1000],          // E N W S
      [1, -1, 0x10000], [-1, -1, 0x100000], [-1, 1, 0x1000000], [1, 1, 0x10000000], // NE NW SW SE
    ];
    // 原版 BlockStyle 双向连通门控（Framing.cs:92-98 + SelfFrame8Way:199-316）：
    // 半砖/坡面按 blockStyleLookup 断开对应方向——half 断上；slope1 断上/右；
    // slope2 断上/左；slope3 断下/右；slope4 断下/左。中心与邻居两侧都要放行
    //（中心 own edge && 邻居 opposite edge；角落需相邻两条边同时放行）。
    // edge mask: bit0=up bit1=down bit2=left bit3=right（blockType 0-5，half=1 slope1-4=2-5）
    const BLOCK_STYLE = [0b1111, 0b1110, 0b0110, 0b1010, 0b0101, 0b1001];
    const edgesOf = (ii: number): number => {
      const b = st.half[ii] ? 1 : (st.slope[ii] >= 1 && st.slope[ii] <= 4 ? st.slope[ii] + 1 : 0);
      return BLOCK_STYLE[b];
    };
    const cE = edgesOf(st.idx(x, y));
    // 方向 (dx,dy) 连通 = 中心各分量 edge && 邻居各反向 edge。
    // 角方向额外要求两个正交邻居的交叉边（SelfFrame8Way:269-316 Clear() 语义：
    // 正交方向未连通时其 BlockStyle 被 Clear → 角检查随之失败）
    const DIR_EDGE: Array<[number, number]> = [
      [0b1000, 0b0100], // E: 中心 right / 邻居 left
      [0b0001, 0b0010], // N: 中心 up / 邻居 down
      [0b0100, 0b1000], // W: 中心 left / 邻居 right
      [0b0010, 0b0001], // S: 中心 down / 邻居 up
      [0b1001, 0b0110], // NE: 中心 up|right / 对角 left|down
      [0b0101, 0b1010], // NW: 中心 up|left / 对角 right|down
      [0b0110, 0b1001], // SW: 中心 down|left / 对角 up|right
      [0b1010, 0b0101], // SE: 中心 down|right / 对角 up|left

}
function solidAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && !!TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]]?.solid;
}
function isDungeonWall(w: number): boolean { return DUNGEON_WALLS.has(w); }
function inWorld(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, slack: number): boolean {
  return x >= slack && y >= slack && x < st.w - slack && y < st.h - slack;
}

/** WorldGen.IsTileNearby（cs:8828）:distance 方框内是否存在该 type */
function isTileNearby(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, type: number, distance: number): boolean {
  for (let i = x - distance; i <= x + distance; i++) {
    for (let j = y - distance; j <= y + distance; j++) {
      if (st.inBounds(i, j) && st.flags[st.idx(i, j)] !== 0 && st.type[st.idx(i, j)] === type) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 活木写入（cs 中 `type=191; active(true); halfBrick(false)` 三连） */
function setLivingWood(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  st.type[i] = LIVING_WOOD;
  st.flags[i] = 1;
  st.half[i] = 0;
}
function setLeaf(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  st.type[i] = LEAF;
  st.flags[i] = 1;
  st.half[i] = 0;
}
function deactivate(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 0;
}

```ts
export function runSurfaceOreAndStonePass(st, rng, gs) {
  const w = st.w;
  let num = rng.int((w * 5 / 4200) | 0, (w * 10 / 4200) | 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;
    while (tries > 0) {
      tries--;
      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);
      while (x >= w * 0.48 && x <= w * 0.52) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);
      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface);
      let near = false;
      for (let j = 0; j < gs.numOrePatch; j++) if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[j]) < 200) near = true;
      if (!near && orePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) {
        if (gs.numOrePatch < 49) { gs.orePatchX[gs.numOrePatch] = x; gs.numOrePatch++; }
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  num = rng.int(1, (w * 7 / 4200) | 0);
  for (let k = 0; k < num; k++) {
    let tries = (w / 420) | 0;
    while (tries > 0) {
      tries--;
      let x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);
      while (x >= w * 0.47 && x <= w * 0.53) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, w - gs.beachDistance);
      const y = rng.int(gs.worldSurfaceLow, gs.worldSurface);
      let near = false;
      for (let l = 0; l < gs.numOrePatch; l++) if (Math.abs(x - gs.orePatchX[l]) < 100) near = true;
      if (!near && stonePatch(st, rng, gs, x, y)) break;
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
function orePatch(st, rng, gs, X, Y): boolean {
  const w = st.w, h = st.h;
  let type = gs.oreTiers.copper;
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) type = gs.oreTiers.iron;
  let num = X, num2 = Y;
  while (!solidTile(st, num, num2)) {
    num2++;
    if (num2 > gs.worldSurface) return false;
  }
  if (!CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num, num2)) || !CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num-1, num2)) || !CONV_GRASS.has(sheetAt(st, num+1, num2))) return false;
  if (wallAt(st, num, num2) > 0) return false;
  for (let i = num - 10; i <= num + 10; i++)
    for (let j = num2 + 7; j <= num2 + 30; j++) {
      const k = idx(i,j); if (!st.flags[k] || TILE_DUNGEON.has(SHEET[st.type[k]]) || CLOUDS.has(...) || CONV_SAND.has(...) || st.wall[k] === 0) return false;
    }
  num2 += rng.int(0, 1);
  setOre(st, num, num2, type); oreHelper(st, num, num2);
  const num3 = num2;
  while (num2 < num3 + rng.int(8, 12)) {
    num += rng.int(-1, 1);
    num2 += rng.int(1, 2);
    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) num2++;
    setOre(st, num, num2, type); oreHelper(st, num, num2);
    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) {
      const num4 = num + rng.int(-2, 2);
      const num5 = num2 + rng.int(1, 1);   // Next(2) → rng.int(1,1)
      setOre(st, num4, num5, type); oreHelper(st, num4, num5);
    }
  }
  // 走向团
  let vx = num, vy = num2;
  let v2x = rng.next() * 0.6 - 0.3;
  let v2y = rng.next() * 0.5 + 0.5;
  let num6 = rng.int(5, 8);
  let num7 = rng.int(9, 13);
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) num6 += rng.int(1, 1);
  if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) num7 += rng.int(1, 1);
  while (num7 > 0) {
    num7--;
    const lim = num6 * 4;
    for (let k = num - (num6 * 4) | 0; k <= num + num6 * 4.0; k++)
      for (let l = num2 - (num6*4)|0; l <= num2 + num6*4.0; l++) {
        const num8 = num6 * (0.5 + rng.next() * 0.5) * 0.1;
        let num9 = num6 * (0.7 + rng.next() * 0.6) * 0.3;
        if (rng.int(0, 7) === 0) num9 *= 2;
        if (!st.inBounds(k, l)) continue;   // Main.tile[k,l] 越界保护
        const dx = vx - k, dy = vy - l;
        const d = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
        const kk = st.idx(k, l);
        if (d < num8) { st.flags[kk] = 0; }
        else if (d < num9) {
          st.type[kk] = type;
          if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) st.flags[kk] = 1;
          oreHelper(st, k, l);
        }
      }
    vx += v2x; vy += v2y;
    v2x += rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1;
    v2y += rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1;
    // 原版 Utils.Clamp(...) 语句无赋值（返回值被丢弃）——不夹紧，1:1 保留
  }
  return true;
}
